# ES-EX saberes — DOE code coverage & gaps

Generated by the one-time `lift_es_ex_saber_codes.py` transform (PR #1905).
The official DOE code of each saber is now stored in the `codigo` field and
every **fully-coded** block is sorted by it. The blocks below still need a
**curriculum-expert pass** (DOE re-extraction follow-up): either a saber has
no DOE code (state-inherited fallback — may be content not in the Extremadura
decree) or the code sequence has a hole (an omitted saber).

- saberes total: **4601** — coded **3399**, uncoded **1202**
- blocks fully coded (sorted by `codigo`): **394**
- blocks needing review: **518**
